<script type="text/javascript">
    if (typeof window.getCookie !== "undefined") {
        var pv = getCookie('et_session_pv');
        var cookieValue = "0";
        if (pv !== null) {
            var pvInt = parseInt(pv);
            pvInt++;
            cookieValue = pvInt.toString();
        }
        var now = new Date();
        var expiration = new Date(now.getTime() + 12 * 3600 * 1000);
        document.cookie = "et_session_pv" + "=" + cookieValue + "; path=/" + "; domain=" + COOKIE_DOMAIN_VISITOR + "; expires=" + expiration.toUTCString();

        if (typeof pageKeyValues !== "undefined") {
            pageKeyValues["session_pv"] = cookieValue;
        }

        var additionalParams = "session_pv=" + cookieValue;
        if (typeof AD_KEY_VALUES_STRING !== "undefined") {
            if (AD_KEY_VALUES_STRING.trim().length !== 0) {
                AD_KEY_VALUES_STRING = AD_KEY_VALUES_STRING + encodeURIComponent("&" + additionalParams);
            } else {
                AD_KEY_VALUES_STRING = encodeURIComponent(additionalParams);
            }
        }
    }
</script>
